⏺ ## What PR #71 achieves

**18 files, +1,428/−154, knowledge-commons 0.5.0 → 0.6.0.**

### The pipeline gains the two stages it was missing

The `/process` model has four stages — take in, preserve, synthesize, extract from the raw — and two were absent from the template. Nothing wrote a source note (one step searched for one, another stamped one); nothing produced a synthesis, though the role was specified into the conventions, the knowledge-graph template, the interview, and the README. Both now exist as named, numbered stages, generated into every pipeline unconditionally — the form varies (small sources inline, large ones archive), the presence doesn't.

### The synthesis is redefined, and the inversion it caused is undone

A synthesis was defined as an *intermediate* between a source and the evidence extracted from it — which put a lossy distillation inside the extraction path, and is likely why it was specified in four places and implemented in none. It's now a **sibling output** of the raw material, corrected in `graph-conventions.md`, the knowledge-graph template, the README, and this project's generated skill.

Concretely for this repo: the chronicle moves from **source tier to synthesis tier**, and the machinery that existed only to contain it is gone — the primary/fallback ordering, the overlapping-tiers guard, and the transcript-survival `grep`. Extraction now reads the transcript, with the reason stated so it survives a rewrite.

### Preservation becomes real, and guarded

Raw material goes to a committed `knowledge/sources/raw/` instead of a gitignored scratch directory. Because this repo is public, that needs a gate: a deterministic credential scan (`scan-secrets.sh`) plus an agent read-through, both required, resolved per hit as redact/withhold/accept, withholding when no human can decide, and failing closed when the scan itself can't run.

### The corrections reach graphs that already exist

Six delta entries, all anchored on headings present in already-generated prose, so none of the six pre-existing anchors break. `graph-init` learns the archive/gate questions and the three-piece synthesis rule. Both version files bump together.

### Two things it does *not* achieve — worth being plain about

**The preserve stage has never archived a real session.** `knowledge/sources/raw/` contains only `.gitkeep`. The gate has been tested extensively in isolation (45 cases, plus a real 100 KB export and a 4.5 MB transcript), but the end-to-end run was out of scope here — you ran `/process` on the authoring session separately, and it landed on main. So the four stages have not been exercised together in one run.

**It has had no human review.** Still a draft. Three automated reviewers found five fail-open defects in the gate, none of which were visible to reading the code — every one needed executing it. That's a reason to weight the gate's test suite over its prose, not a substitute for you looking at it.

### A byproduct worth more than it looks

The work surfaced a live resolver defect: a session's transcript gets one home however many projects it spans, so a session doing this project's work can sit under another project's slug and be invisible to both the queue and the resolver. That's now written into the skill as *search by UUID across all slugs, never by project glob* — and it's the error that produced the false "founding transcript was lost" claim, which I repeated and then confirmed with a search using the very glob that couldn't have found it.

⏺ Bash(cd /Users/derek-personal/Developer/claude-code-plugins
python3 - <<'PY'
import yaml
P="/Users/derek-personal/.claude/plugins/cache/ddehart-plugins/knowledge-commons/0.6.0"

pending=[]; applied=[]; oos=[]
for e in log:
    f=e["file"]
    if not present.get(f): oos.append(e); continue
    sub=gen.get(f) if gen else None
    lst=(sub or {}).get("applied",[])
    (applied if e["id"] in lst else pending).append(e)

for f in ("process","knowledge-graph"):

if not pending: print("  none")
print(f"\nout of scope ({len(oos)}): {[e['id'] for e in oos] or 'none'}")

stamped={i for f in ('process','knowledge-graph') for i in (gen or {}).get(f,{}).get('applied',[])}
ids={e['id'] for e in log}

wc -lw "$OUT" 2>/dev/null | awk '{print "  ", $1, "lines,", $2, "words"}'
echo "  location: outside repo? $(case "$OUT" in /Users/derek-personal/Developer/claude-code-plugins/*) echo NO;; *) echo yes;; esac)")
